Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the complexity object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 872. The moduli space has dimension at most 572 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_872(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 872. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^572(S, F) is trivial. QED.
